- **[warn] Account, Contact, npsp__Account_Soft_Credit__c all map to Account.**
  - Why: Several source objects writing to one target object can double-write records, and the reconciliation config for that target is taken from whichever mapping is found first.

## NPSP Configuration
### Migration-relevant settings
| Setting | Value | Why it matters |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Account model | Household Account | Decides the Contact/Household → Person Account transform |
| Enhanced Recurring Donations | yes (RD2) | RD schedule shape differs between RD1 and RD2 |
| Payments enabled | no | Whether Opportunity Payments must fold into GiftTransaction |
| Default GAU | — | Fallback fund for unallocated gifts |
| Household rules | No Contacts | Affects household grouping/naming |
